   305	        rawEvidenceLength: reLen,
   306	        generationToken: gt,
   307	        contextVersion: cv,
   308	      },
   309	    };
   310	  }
   311	
   312	  // RN-06 closure: resolve opts.clock AFTER envelope validation; descriptor-safe; throwing getter
   313	  // collapses to createdAt = null (clock is best-effort; never blocks the build).
   314	  function _resolveClock(opts) {
   315	    if (opts === null || opts === undefined) return null;
   316	    if (!RC.isOriginalPlainObject(opts)) return null;
   317	    if (RC.hasHiddenOwnKey(opts)) return null;
   318	    if (!_hasOnlyAllowedKeys(opts, OPTS_KEYS)) return null;
   319	    var desc;
   320	    try { desc = Object.getOwnPropertyDescriptor(opts, 'clock'); }
   321	    catch (e) { return null; }
   322	    if (!desc) return null;
   323	    if (!('value' in desc)) return null;       // accessor descriptor — fail-soft
   324	    var clock = desc.value;
   325	    if (typeof clock !== 'function') return null;
   326	    try {
   327	      var v = clock();
   328	      if (v === null) return null;
   329	      if (!_isIsoTimestamp(v)) return null;
   330	      return v;
   331	    } catch (e) { return null; }
   332	  }
   333	
   334	  // DFS cycle detection over the directed CAUSAL edge list. Uses a proto-null colour map (RN-08).
   335	  function _findCycle(nodeIds, adjacencyCausal) {
   336	    var WHITE = 0, GREY = 1, BLACK = 2;
   337	    var color = Object.create(null);
   338	    for (var i = 0; i < nodeIds.length; i++) color[nodeIds[i]] = WHITE;
   339	    function visit(u) {
   340	      color[u] = GREY;
   341	      var nbrs = adjacencyCausal[u] || [];
   342	      for (var k = 0; k < nbrs.length; k++) {
   343	        var v = nbrs[k];
   344	        if (color[v] === GREY) return v;
   345	        if (color[v] === WHITE) {
   346	          var c = visit(v);
   347	          if (c !== null) return c;
   348	        }
   349	      }
   350	      color[u] = BLACK;
   351	      return null;
   352	    }
   353	    for (var j = 0; j < nodeIds.length; j++) {
   354	      var n = nodeIds[j];
   355	      if (color[n] === WHITE) {
   356	        var hit = visit(n);
   357	        if (hit !== null) return hit;
   358	      }
   359	    }
   360	    return null;
   361	  }
   362	
   363	  // Kahn's algorithm — deterministic by tie-breaking on sorted nodeId. Assumes no cycle. Uses
   364	  // proto-null in-degree map (RN-08).
   365	  function _topologicalOrder(nodeIds, adjacencyCausal) {
   366	    var inDegree = Object.create(null);
   367	    var i;
   368	    for (i = 0; i < nodeIds.length; i++) inDegree[nodeIds[i]] = 0;
   369	    for (i = 0; i < nodeIds.length; i++) {
   370	      var nbrs = adjacencyCausal[nodeIds[i]] || [];
   371	      for (var k = 0; k < nbrs.length; k++) {
   372	        if (Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(inDegree, nbrs[k])) inDegree[nbrs[k]]++;
   373	      }
   374	    }
   375	    var queue = [];
   376	    for (i = 0; i < nodeIds.length; i++) if (inDegree[nodeIds[i]] === 0) queue.push(nodeIds[i]);
   377	    queue.sort(_strcmp);
   378	    var out = [];
   379	    while (queue.length > 0) {
   380	      var u = queue.shift();
   381	      out.push(u);
   382	      var nbrs2 = (adjacencyCausal[u] || []).slice().sort(_strcmp);
   383	      for (var m = 0; m < nbrs2.length; m++) {
   384	        var v = nbrs2[m];
   385	        if (Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(inDegree, v)) {
   386	          inDegree[v]--;
   387	          if (inDegree[v] === 0) {
   388	            var lo = 0, hi = queue.length;
   389	            while (lo < hi) { var mid = (lo + hi) >>> 1; if (queue[mid] < v) lo = mid + 1; else hi = mid; }
   390	            queue.splice(lo, 0, v);
   391	          }
   392	        }
   393	      }
   394	    }
   395	    return out;
   396	  }
   397	
   398	  // Materialise the final closed-shape sanitized graph (deep freeze every nested array/object).
   399	  function _materializeGraph(o) {
   400	    // Deep freeze correlationGroups (RN-05): freeze memberNodeIds BEFORE the enclosing group.
   401	    var cgFrozen = o.correlationGroups.map(function (g) {
   402	      return Object.freeze({
   403	        correlationGroupId: g.correlationGroupId,
   404	        memberNodeIds: Object.freeze(g.memberNodeIds.slice()),
   405	        independenceWeight: g.independenceWeight,
   406	      });
   407	    });
   408	    var dedupSummaryFrozen = Object.freeze({
   409	      rejectedDuplicateIds: Object.freeze(o.dedup.rejectedDuplicateIds.slice()),
   410	      rejectedSemanticDuplicates: Object.freeze(o.dedup.rejectedSemanticDuplicates.map(function (r) {
   411	        return Object.freeze({
   412	          fingerprint: r.fingerprint,
   413	          keptNodeId: r.keptNodeId,
   414	          droppedNodeId: r.droppedNodeId,
   415	        });
   416	      })),
   417	      rejectedSourceReplays: Object.freeze(o.dedup.rejectedSourceReplays.map(function (r) { return Object.freeze(Object.assign({}, r)); })),
   418	    });
   419	    var graph = {
   420	      schemaVersion: GRAPH_SCHEMA_VERSION,
   421	      graphId: o.graphId,
   422	      caseAssociation: Object.freeze({
   423	        caseId: o.caseAssociation.caseId,
   424	        sessionId: o.caseAssociation.sessionId,
   425	        lapId: o.caseAssociation.lapId == null ? null : o.caseAssociation.lapId,
   426	      }),
   427	      sessionAssociation: Object.freeze({ sessionId: o.caseAssociation.sessionId }),
   428	      nodes: Object.freeze(o.nodes.slice()),
   429	      edges: Object.freeze(o.edges.slice()),
   430	      topologicalOrder: Object.freeze(o.topologicalOrder.slice()),
   431	      deduplicationSummary: dedupSummaryFrozen,
   432	      correlationGroups: Object.freeze(cgFrozen),
   433	      limitations: Object.freeze(o.limitations.slice()),
   434	      cannotConclude: Object.freeze(o.cannotConclude.slice()),
   435	      provenance: Object.freeze({
   436	        builderVersion: SERVICE_VERSION,
   437	        inputCount: o.provenance.inputCount,
   438	        sanitizedCount: o.provenance.sanitizedCount,
   439	        rejectedCount: o.provenance.rejectedCount,
   440	        rejectedReasonsSummary: Object.freeze(Object.assign({}, o.provenance.rejectedReasonsSummary)),
   441	      }),
   442	      createdAt: o.createdAt,
   443	      generationToken: o.generationToken,
   444	      contextVersion: o.contextVersion,
   445	    };
   446	    return Object.freeze(graph);
   447	  }
   448	
   449	  /**
   450	   * buildEvidenceGraph(input, opts) — primary entry. See module header for the contract.
   451	   */
   452	  function buildEvidenceGraph(inputIn, optsIn) {
   453	    try {
   454	      // Step 1 — input envelope structural gate + TRUSTED SNAPSHOT (RN-01 closure).
   455	      var env = _validateInputEnvelope(inputIn);
   456	      if (!env.ok) return env;
   457	      var caseAssociation = env.snapshot.caseAssociation;
   458	      var generationToken = env.snapshot.generationToken;
   459	      var contextVersion = env.snapshot.contextVersion;
   460	      var rawEvidenceRef = env.snapshot.rawEvidenceRef;
   461	      var inputCount = env.snapshot.rawEvidenceLength;
   462	
   463	      // Step 2 — resolve clock AFTER envelope validation (RN-06 closure).
   464	      var createdAt = _resolveClock(optsIn);
   465	
   466	      // Step 3 — per-node validation. Each rawEvidence[i] is read via descriptor (RN-02 closure):
   467	      // accessor / sparse / inherited slots become ONE rejected node, never a whole-build throw.
   468	      var sanitized = [];
   469	      var rejectedCount = 0;
   470	      var rejectedReasons = Object.create(null);
   471	      function tally(code) { rejectedReasons[code] = (rejectedReasons[code] || 0) + 1; }
   472	
   473	      for (var i = 0; i < inputCount; i++) {
   474	        var desc;
   475	        try { desc = Object.getOwnPropertyDescriptor(rawEvidenceRef, i); }
   476	        catch (e) { rejectedCount++; tally(CODES.PROTOTYPE_POLLUTION_REJECTED); continue; }
   477	        if (!desc) { rejectedCount++; tally(CODES.PROTOTYPE_POLLUTION_REJECTED); continue; }
   478	        if (!('value' in desc)) { rejectedCount++; tally(CODES.PROTOTYPE_POLLUTION_REJECTED); continue; }
   479	        var raw = desc.value;
   480	        // outer defence — refuse non-plain prototype BEFORE invoking D1 validator.
   481	        if (!RC.isOriginalPlainObject(raw)) { rejectedCount++; tally(CODES.PROTOTYPE_POLLUTION_REJECTED); continue; }
   482	        if (RC.hasHiddenOwnKey(raw)) { rejectedCount++; tally(CODES.UNKNOWN_OWN_KEY); continue; }
   483	        if (RC.hasNonPlainNestedObject(raw)) { rejectedCount++; tally(CODES.PROTOTYPE_POLLUTION_REJECTED); continue; }
   484	        var check = EN.validateEvidenceNodeShape(raw);
   485	        if (check.valid !== true) {
   486	          rejectedCount++;
   487	          var codes = Array.isArray(check.reasonCodes) ? check.reasonCodes : [CODES.EVIDENCE_NODE_INVALID];
   488	          for (var rci = 0; rci < codes.length; rci++) tally(codes[rci]);
   489	          continue;
   490	        }
   491	        var san = check.sanitized;
   492	        // RN-09 — per-observation params key count cap (defence layer above D1).
   493	        if (san.observation && san.observation.params && typeof san.observation.params === 'object') {
   494	          try {
   495	            var pkc = Object.keys(san.observation.params).length;
   496	            if (pkc > PARAMS_KEYS_CAP) { rejectedCount++; tally(CODES.ARRAY_CAP_EXCEEDED); continue; }
   497	          } catch (e) { rejectedCount++; tally(CODES.PROTOTYPE_POLLUTION_REJECTED); continue; }
   498	        }
   499	        // Association binding — caseId / sessionId MUST equal envelope.caseAssociation.
   500	        if (san.identity.caseId !== caseAssociation.caseId) { rejectedCount++; tally(CODES.SOURCE_IDENTITY_CASE_MISMATCH); continue; }
   501	        if (san.identity.sessionId !== caseAssociation.sessionId) { rejectedCount++; tally(CODES.SOURCE_IDENTITY_SESSION_MISMATCH); continue; }
   502	        if (caseAssociation.lapId !== null && san.identity.lapId !== null && san.identity.lapId !== caseAssociation.lapId) {
   503	          rejectedCount++; tally(CODES.SOURCE_IDENTITY_LAP_MISMATCH); continue;
   504	        }
   505	        // Imported-summary elevation guard.
   506	        if (san.identity.sourceId === IMPORTED_SUMMARY_SOURCE_ID && san.credibility === 'measured') {
   507	          rejectedCount++; tally(CODES.EVIDENCE_IMPORTED_SUMMARY_ELEVATION_FORBIDDEN); continue;
   508	        }
   509	        sanitized.push(san);
   510	      }
   511	
   512	      // Step 4 — cap on accepted nodes.
   513	      if (sanitized.length > NODES_CAP) {
   514	        return RC.buildBlockedResult([CODES.GRAPH_CAP_EXCEEDED], { detail: 'sanitized node count ' + sanitized.length + ' exceeds NODES_CAP=' + NODES_CAP });
   515	      }
   516	
   517	      // Step 5 — RN-03 closure: sort sanitized by full canonical content BEFORE any dedup so caller
   518	      // permutation cannot influence which duplicate wins. Tie-breaker on a deterministic field.
   519	      sanitized.sort(function (a, b) { return _strcmp(_fullCanonicalString(a), _fullCanonicalString(b)); });
   520	
   521	      // Step 6 — duplicate-ID dedup (Object.create(null) map — RN-08 closure). First by sort order
   522	      // (deterministic) is kept; subsequent rejected.
   523	      var byId = Object.create(null);
   524	      var keptById = [];
   525	      var rejectedDuplicateIds = [];
   526	      for (var d = 0; d < sanitized.length; d++) {
   527	        var n = sanitized[d];
   528	        if (Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(byId, n.nodeId)) {
   529	          rejectedDuplicateIds.push(n.nodeId);
   530	          tally(CODES.EVIDENCE_DUPLICATE_ID);
   531	          rejectedCount++;
   532	          continue;
   533	        }
   534	        byId[n.nodeId] = n;
   535	        keptById.push(n);
   536	      }
   537	      rejectedDuplicateIds.sort(_strcmp);
   538	
   539	      // Step 7 — semantic CANONICAL STRING dedup (RN-04 closure). The map KEY is the full canonical
   540	      // string itself, not a hash. Collisions are impossible by definition.
   541	      var semSeen = Object.create(null);
   542	      var keptAfterSemantic = [];
   543	      var rejectedSemanticDuplicates = [];
   544	      for (var s = 0; s < keptById.length; s++) {
   545	        var nn = keptById[s];
   546	        var canon = _semanticCanonicalString(nn);
   547	        if (Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(semSeen, canon)) {
   548	          rejectedSemanticDuplicates.push({ fingerprint: _hash64('sem|' + canon), keptNodeId: semSeen[canon], droppedNodeId: nn.nodeId });
   549	          tally(CODES.EVIDENCE_GRAPH_DUPLICATED_SOURCE_DOUBLECOUNT);
   550	          rejectedCount++;
   551	          delete byId[nn.nodeId];
   552	          continue;
   553	        }
   554	        semSeen[canon] = nn.nodeId;
   555	        keptAfterSemantic.push(nn);
   556	      }
   557	      rejectedSemanticDuplicates.sort(function (a, b) { return _strcmp(a.droppedNodeId, b.droppedNodeId); });
   558	
   559	      // Step 8 — orphan edge detection. Any supportingEdges / contradictingEdges target nodeId that
   560	      // is not in the surviving graph triggers EVIDENCE_GRAPH_ORPHAN.
   561	      var orphanDetected = false;
   562	      var orphanDetail = null;
   563	      for (var e = 0; e < keptAfterSemantic.length; e++) {
   564	        var nx = keptAfterSemantic[e];
   565	        var allEdges = nx.supportingEdges.concat(nx.contradictingEdges);
   566	        for (var ek = 0; ek < allEdges.length; ek++) {
   567	          var tgt = allEdges[ek];
   568	          if (!Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(byId, tgt)) {
   569	            orphanDetected = true;
   570	            orphanDetail = 'node ' + nx.nodeId + ' references unknown nodeId ' + tgt;
   571	            break;
   572	          }
   573	        }
   574	        if (orphanDetected) break;
   575	      }
   576	      if (orphanDetected) {
   577	        return RC.buildBlockedResult([CODES.EVIDENCE_GRAPH_ORPHAN], { detail: orphanDetail });
   578	      }
   579	
   580	      // Step 9 — build edges + causal adjacency (RN-08: proto-null map).
   581	      var edges = [];
   582	      var adjacencyCausal = Object.create(null);
   583	      function addEdge(from, to, kind) {
   584	        if (EDGE_KIND_ALLOWED.indexOf(kind) === -1) return false;
   585	        edges.push({ from: from, to: to, kind: kind });
   586	        if (EDGE_KIND_CAUSAL[kind]) {
   587	          if (!adjacencyCausal[from]) adjacencyCausal[from] = [];
   588	          adjacencyCausal[from].push(to);
   589	        }
   590	        return true;
   591	      }
   592	      for (var f = 0; f < keptAfterSemantic.length; f++) {
   593	        var nf = keptAfterSemantic[f];
   594	        for (var sf = 0; sf < nf.supportingEdges.length; sf++) {
   595	          if (nf.supportingEdges[sf] === nf.nodeId) {
   596	            return RC.buildBlockedResult([CODES.EVIDENCE_GRAPH_SELF_REFERENCE], { detail: nf.nodeId + ' supportingEdges self-reference' });
   597	          }
   598	          addEdge(nf.nodeId, nf.supportingEdges[sf], 'supports');
   599	        }
   600	        for (var cf = 0; cf < nf.contradictingEdges.length; cf++) {
   601	          if (nf.contradictingEdges[cf] === nf.nodeId) {
   602	            return RC.buildBlockedResult([CODES.EVIDENCE_GRAPH_SELF_REFERENCE], { detail: nf.nodeId + ' contradictingEdges self-reference' });
   603	          }
   604	          addEdge(nf.nodeId, nf.contradictingEdges[cf], 'contradicts');
   605	        }
   606	      }
   607	
   608	      // Step 10 — correlation groups. Same canonical (caseId|sessionId|lapId|sourceId) → one group.
   609	      var groupBucket = Object.create(null);
   610	      for (var g = 0; g < keptAfterSemantic.length; g++) {
   611	        var ng = keptAfterSemantic[g];
   612	        var gk = _correlationGroupCanonical(ng);
   613	        if (!groupBucket[gk]) groupBucket[gk] = [];
   614	        groupBucket[gk].push(ng.nodeId);
   615	      }
   616	      var groupKeysSorted = Object.keys(groupBucket).sort(_strcmp);
   617	      var correlationGroups = [];
   618	      for (var gi = 0; gi < groupKeysSorted.length; gi++) {
   619	        var gk2 = groupKeysSorted[gi];
   620	        var members = groupBucket[gk2].slice().sort(_strcmp);
   620	        var members = groupBucket[gk2].slice().sort(_strcmp);
   621	        var iw = members.length > 0 ? 1 / members.length : 0;
   622	        correlationGroups.push({
   623	          correlationGroupId: _correlationGroupId(gk2),
   624	          memberNodeIds: members,
   625	          independenceWeight: iw,
   626	        });
   627	        if (members.length >= 2) {
   628	          for (var mi = 0; mi < members.length; mi++) {
   629	            for (var mj = mi + 1; mj < members.length; mj++) {
   630	              var from = members[mi] < members[mj] ? members[mi] : members[mj];
   631	              var to = members[mi] < members[mj] ? members[mj] : members[mi];
   632	              addEdge(from, to, 'correlated_with');
   633	            }
   634	          }
   635	        }
   636	      }
   637	      if (correlationGroups.length > CORRELATION_GROUPS_CAP) {
   638	        return RC.buildBlockedResult([CODES.GRAPH_CAP_EXCEEDED], { detail: 'correlation groups ' + correlationGroups.length + ' exceeds CORRELATION_GROUPS_CAP=' + CORRELATION_GROUPS_CAP });
   639	      }
   640	
   641	      // Step 11 — edge cap.
   642	      if (edges.length > EDGES_CAP_TOTAL) {
   643	        return RC.buildBlockedResult([CODES.GRAPH_CAP_EXCEEDED], { detail: 'edges ' + edges.length + ' exceeds EDGES_CAP_TOTAL=' + EDGES_CAP_TOTAL });
   644	      }
   645	
   646	      // Step 12 — cycle detection over CAUSAL edges only.
   647	      var sortedNodeIds = keptAfterSemantic.map(function (x) { return x.nodeId; }).slice().sort(_strcmp);
   648	      var cycleHit = _findCycle(sortedNodeIds, adjacencyCausal);
   649	      if (cycleHit !== null) {
   650	        return RC.buildBlockedResult([CODES.EVIDENCE_GRAPH_CYCLE], { detail: 'cycle involves nodeId ' + cycleHit });
   651	      }
   652	
   653	      // Step 13 — topological order over the acyclic causal subgraph.
   654	      var topo = _topologicalOrder(sortedNodeIds, adjacencyCausal);
   655	
   656	      // Step 14 — sort nodes deterministically; sort edges by (from, to, kind).
   657	      var nodesOut = keptAfterSemantic.slice().sort(function (a, b) { return _strcmp(a.nodeId, b.nodeId); });
   658	      var edgesOut = edges.slice().sort(function (a, b) {
   659	        var c = _strcmp(a.from, b.from); if (c !== 0) return c;
   660	        c = _strcmp(a.to, b.to); if (c !== 0) return c;
   661	        return _strcmp(a.kind, b.kind);
   662	      });
   663	      for (var ni = 0; ni < nodesOut.length; ni++) Object.freeze(nodesOut[ni]);
   664	      for (var ei = 0; ei < edgesOut.length; ei++) Object.freeze(edgesOut[ei]);
   665	
   666	      // Step 15 — limitations / cannotConclude derivation.
   667	      var limitations = [];
   668	      var cannotConclude = [];
   669	      var limSeen = Object.create(null);
   670	      for (var li = 0; li < nodesOut.length; li++) {
   671	        var lims = nodesOut[li].limitations;
   672	        for (var lj = 0; lj < lims.length; lj++) {
   673	          if (!limSeen[lims[lj]]) { limSeen[lims[lj]] = true; limitations.push(lims[lj]); }
   674	        }
   675	      }
   676	      if (nodesOut.length === 0) {
   677	        if (cannotConclude.indexOf(CODES.INSUFFICIENT_EVIDENCE) === -1) cannotConclude.push(CODES.INSUFFICIENT_EVIDENCE);
   678	      }
   679	      if (rejectedCount > 0) {
   680	        if (cannotConclude.indexOf(CODES.CANNOT_CONCLUDE) === -1) cannotConclude.push(CODES.CANNOT_CONCLUDE);
   681	      }
   682	      limitations.sort(_strcmp);
   683	      cannotConclude.sort(_strcmp);
   684	      if (limitations.length > LIMITATIONS_CAP) limitations = limitations.slice(0, LIMITATIONS_CAP);
   685	      if (cannotConclude.length > CANNOT_CONCLUDE_CAP) cannotConclude = cannotConclude.slice(0, CANNOT_CONCLUDE_CAP);
   686	
   687	      // Step 16 — derive graphId (content-only; clock-independent).
   688	      var idPayload = {
   689	        caseAssociation: caseAssociation,
   690	        nodes: nodesOut.map(function (n) {
   691	          return {
   692	            nodeId: n.nodeId,
   693	            category: n.category,
   694	            credibility: n.credibility,
   695	            provenance: n.provenance,
   696	            availability: n.availability,
   697	            identity: {
   698	              caseId: n.identity.caseId,
   699	              sessionId: n.identity.sessionId,
   700	              lapId: n.identity.lapId,
   701	              sourceId: n.identity.sourceId,
   702	              sourceVersion: n.identity.sourceVersion,
   703	            },
   704	            observation: {
   705	              kind: n.observation.kind,
   706	              channel: n.observation.channel,
   707	              i18nKey: n.observation.i18nKey,
   708	              params: n.observation.params,
   709	            },
   710	            limitations: n.limitations,
   711	            supportingEdges: n.supportingEdges,
   712	            contradictingEdges: n.contradictingEdges,
   713	          };
   714	        }),
   715	        edges: edgesOut,
   716	        correlationGroups: correlationGroups.map(function (g) { return { correlationGroupId: g.correlationGroupId, memberNodeIds: g.memberNodeIds, independenceWeight: g.independenceWeight }; }),
   717	      };
   718	      var graphId = 'graph_' + _hash64('graphid|v' + GRAPH_SCHEMA_VERSION + '|' + _stableStringify(idPayload));
   719	
   720	      // Step 17 — RN-09 closure: post-sanitization JSON byte cap. Safe to JSON.stringify because every
   721	      // sanitized value is a plain frozen primitive (no accessors, no Proxies, no class instances).
   722	      var envBytes;
   723	      try {
   724	        var s = JSON.stringify(idPayload);
   725	        envBytes = (typeof TextEncoder !== 'undefined') ? new TextEncoder().encode(s).length : Buffer.byteLength(s, 'utf8');
   726	      } catch (e) { envBytes = Infinity; }
   727	      if (envBytes > ENVELOPE_BYTE_CAP) {
   728	        return RC.buildBlockedResult([CODES.BYTE_CAP_EXCEEDED], { detail: 'post-sanitization JSON size ' + envBytes + ' exceeds ENVELOPE_BYTE_CAP=' + ENVELOPE_BYTE_CAP });
   729	      }
   730	
   731	      // Step 18 — final materialisation
   732	      var graph = _materializeGraph({
   733	        graphId: graphId,
   734	        caseAssociation: caseAssociation,
   735	        nodes: nodesOut,
   736	        edges: edgesOut,
   737	        topologicalOrder: topo,
   738	        dedup: {
   739	          rejectedDuplicateIds: rejectedDuplicateIds,
   740	          rejectedSemanticDuplicates: rejectedSemanticDuplicates,
   741	          rejectedSourceReplays: [],
   742	        },
   743	        correlationGroups: correlationGroups,
   744	        limitations: limitations,
   745	        cannotConclude: cannotConclude,
   746	        provenance: {
   747	          inputCount: inputCount,
   748	          sanitizedCount: nodesOut.length,
   749	          rejectedCount: rejectedCount,
   750	          rejectedReasonsSummary: rejectedReasons,
   751	        },
   752	        createdAt: createdAt,
   753	        generationToken: generationToken,
   754	        contextVersion: contextVersion,
   755	      });
   756	
   757	      return Object.freeze({ valid: true, graph: graph });
   758	    } catch (e) {
   759	      return RC.buildBlockedResult([CODES.INTERNAL_CONTRACT_VIOLATION], { detail: 'buildEvidenceGraph threw on hostile input' });
   760	    }
   761	  }
   762	
   763	  // Public API surface — RN-07 closure: internal helpers (_stableStringify, _semanticCanonicalString,
   764	  // _correlationGroupCanonical) are NOT exported. The only public entry is buildEvidenceGraph plus
   765	  // documented constants. Tests must drive the contract via buildEvidenceGraph only.
     1	/**
     2	 * tests/r3.0d-evidence-graph.test.js — R3.0D D2 · Evidence Graph adversarial suite.
     3	 *
     4	 * Verifies the D2 builder:
     5	 *   • valid graph (golden path)
     6	 *   • empty graph policy (returns valid with INSUFFICIENT_EVIDENCE cannotConclude)
     7	 *   • duplicate nodeId rejection (first kept, accounting recorded)
     8	 *   • semantic-fingerprint dedup (same source content under different nodeId)
     9	 *   • correlation grouping (same source-identity grouped, independenceWeight = 1/N)
    10	 *   • directed cycle / multi-node cycle → fail-closed EVIDENCE_GRAPH_CYCLE
    11	 *   • self-edge (post-rename smuggle attempt) → fail-closed EVIDENCE_GRAPH_SELF_REFERENCE
    12	 *   • orphan target edge → fail-closed EVIDENCE_GRAPH_ORPHAN
    13	 *   • stale freshness still passes D1 (shape only); association mismatch (wrong case/session/lap)
    14	 *   • imported_summary elevation guard → EVIDENCE_IMPORTED_SUMMARY_ELEVATION_FORBIDDEN
    15	 *   • forged provenance / forged credibility (D1 rejects synthetic / heuristic without limitation)
    16	 *   • prototype-pollution / Proxy ownKeys / throwing getter / accessor descriptor / Symbol key /
    17	 *     non-enumerable key / class instance / nested class / Array subclass / mutated array prototype /
    18	 *     cyclic caller input / NaN / Infinity / oversized string / oversized array / oversized graph
    19	 *   • deterministic node + edge + topological order + serialization
    20	 *   • deep freeze (caller cannot mutate returned graph)
    21	 *   • caller mutates input array after build → graph unchanged (no shared reference)
    22	 *   • raw telemetry tunnel attempt (params contains oversized string) → BYTE_CAP_EXCEEDED via D1
    23	 *   • private path / filename-shaped nodeId → EVIDENCE_NODE_ID_FORBIDDEN via D1
    24	 *   • future schema rejection (schemaVersion > SUPPORTED_SCHEMA_VERSION) via D1
    25	 *   • injectable clock; missing clock → createdAt null; throwing clock → null fail-closed
    26	 *
    27	 * Node CLI: `node tests/r3.0d-evidence-graph.test.js`, exit 1 on any failure.
    28	 */
    29	'use strict';

    33	const CR = C.credibility;
    34	const EN = C.evidenceNode;
    35	const CODES = RC.REASON_CODES;

    38	let pass = 0, fail = 0;
    39	function chk(name, cond, detail) {
    40	  if (cond) pass++;
    41	  else {
    42	    fail++;
    43	    console.log('  ✗ ' + name + (detail !== undefined ? '  ' + (typeof detail === 'string' ? detail : JSON.stringify(detail)) : ''));
    44	  }
    45	}

    48	const NOW2 = '2026-06-28T06:05:00Z';

    51	function freshId(over) {

    55	    lapId: 'L1',
    56	    sourceId: 'lap_authority',
    57	    sourceVersion: 'v1',
    58	    freshness: NOW,
    59	  }, over || {});
    60	}

    63	  return Object.assign({

    66	    category: 'data_quality',

    68	    credibility: 'measured',
    69	    provenance: 'real',
    70	    availability: 'available',
    71	    confidence: { state: 'unresolved' },
    72	    observation: { kind: 'channel_missing', i18nKey: 'r3_0d.evidence.channel.missing', params: { channel: 'speed' }, channel: 'speed' },
    73	    limitations: [],
    74	    supportingEdges: [],
    75	    contradictingEdges: [],
    76	  }, over || {});
    77	}

    87	(function sectionA() {
    88	  const out = EG.buildEvidenceGraph(freshInput(), { clock: CLOCK });
    89	  chk('A1 golden path valid', out.valid === true);
    90	  chk('A1a graph.schemaVersion = 1', out.graph && out.graph.schemaVersion === 1);
    91	  chk('A1b graph.graphId starts with graph_', out.graph && /^graph_/.test(out.graph.graphId));
    92	  chk('A1c graph frozen', Object.isFrozen(out.graph));
    93	  chk('A1d graph.nodes frozen', Object.isFrozen(out.graph.nodes));
    94	  chk('A1e graph.edges frozen', Object.isFrozen(out.graph.edges));
    95	  chk('A1f graph.caseAssociation frozen', Object.isFrozen(out.graph.caseAssociation));

    97	  chk('A1h graph.correlationGroups frozen', Object.isFrozen(out.graph.correlationGroups));
    98	  chk('A1i graph.provenance frozen', Object.isFrozen(out.graph.provenance));

   100	  chk('A1k graph.nodes length 1', out.graph.nodes.length === 1);
   101	  chk('A1l one correlation group, weight 1', out.graph.correlationGroups.length === 1 && out.graph.correlationGroups[0].independenceWeight === 1);
   102	  chk('A1m sessionAssociation present', out.graph.sessionAssociation.sessionId === 'sess_demo');
   103	  chk('A1n provenance.inputCount 1', out.graph.provenance.inputCount === 1);
   104	  chk('A1o provenance.sanitizedCount 1', out.graph.provenance.sanitizedCount === 1);
   105	
   106	  // empty rawEvidence — valid graph, INSUFFICIENT_EVIDENCE cannotConclude
   107	  const emptyOut = EG.buildEvidenceGraph(freshInput({ rawEvidence: [] }), { clock: CLOCK });

   110	  chk('A2b empty nodes []', emptyOut.graph.nodes.length === 0);
   111	  chk('A2c empty edges []', emptyOut.graph.edges.length === 0);
   112	})();

   251	(function sectionD() {
   252	  chk('D1 node case mismatch rejected', (function () {
   253	    const n = freshNode({ identity: freshId({ caseId: 'case_other' }) });
   254	    const r = EG.buildEvidenceGraph(freshInput({ rawEvidence: [n] }), { clock: CLOCK });
   255	    return r.valid === true && r.graph.provenance.rejectedReasonsSummary[CODES.SOURCE_IDENTITY_CASE_MISMATCH] === 1;
   256	  })());
   257	  chk('D2 node session mismatch rejected', (function () {
   258	    const n = freshNode({ identity: freshId({ sessionId: 'sess_other' }) });
   259	    const r = EG.buildEvidenceGraph(freshInput({ rawEvidence: [n] }), { clock: CLOCK });
   260	    return r.valid === true && r.graph.provenance.rejectedReasonsSummary[CODES.SOURCE_IDENTITY_SESSION_MISMATCH] === 1;
     1	/**
     2	 * tests/r3-0-phase-no-consumer.test.js — R3.0 Integrated Delivery Train per-phase no-runtime-consumer.
     3	 *
     4	 * Covers scripts/check-r3-phase-no-consumer.js for R3.0D / R3.0E / R3.0F. Validates the real-repo PASS
     5	 * path AND adversarial FAIL fixtures (R3_PHASE_NO_CONSUMER_BASE_OVERRIDE points to synthetic trees so
     6	 * the real renderer/js/ is never mutated).
     7	 */
     8	'use strict';
     9	const fs = require('fs');
    10	const path = require('path');
    11	const cp = require('child_process');
    12	const os = require('os');
    13	
    14	const REPO = path.resolve(__dirname, '..');
    15	const SCRIPT = 'scripts/check-r3-phase-no-consumer.js';
    16	const PHASES = ['R3.0D', 'R3.0E', 'R3.0F'];
    17	const ARTIFACT = { 'R3.0D': 'r3-0d-no-consumer.json', 'R3.0E': 'r3-0e-no-consumer.json', 'R3.0F': 'r3-0f-no-consumer.json' };
    18	const CONTRACT_PREFIX = { 'R3.0D': 'contracts/r3.0d', 'R3.0E': 'contracts/r3.0e', 'R3.0F': 'contracts/r3.0f' };
    19	
    20	let pass = 0, fail = 0;
    21	function chk(name, cond, detail) {
    22	  if (cond) pass++;
    23	  else { fail++; console.log('  FAIL ' + name + (detail !== undefined ? '  ' + (typeof detail === 'string' ? detail : JSON.stringify(detail)) : '')); }
    24	}
    25	
    26	function runValidator(phase, scanBase) {
    27	  const tmp = fs.mkdtempSync(path.join(os.tmpdir(), 'r3-phase-noc-art-'));
    28	  const env = Object.assign({}, process.env, { ARTIFACT_DIR: tmp, R3_PHASE_PROGRAM: phase });
    29	  if (scanBase) env.R3_PHASE_NO_CONSUMER_BASE_OVERRIDE = scanBase;
    30	  const r = cp.spawnSync('node', [SCRIPT], { cwd: REPO, encoding: 'utf8', env });
    31	  let artifact = null;
    32	  try { artifact = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(path.join(tmp, ARTIFACT[phase] || 'r3-phase-no-consumer.json'), 'utf8')); } catch (_) { artifact = null; }
    33	  return { status: r.status, artifact, stderr: r.stderr || '', stdout: r.stdout || '', tmp };
    34	}
    35	
    36	function buildSyntheticTree(consumerLine) {
    37	  const dir = fs.mkdtempSync(path.join(os.tmpdir(), 'r3-phase-noc-tree-'));
    38	  fs.mkdirSync(path.join(dir, 'renderer', 'js'), { recursive: true });
    39	  fs.mkdirSync(path.join(dir, 'renderer'), { recursive: true });
    40	  fs.writeFileSync(path.join(dir, 'renderer', 'js', 'maybe-consumer.js'), consumerLine || '// no consumer\nmodule.exports = {};\n');
    41	  fs.writeFileSync(path.join(dir, 'renderer', 'index.html'), '<!doctype html><html><body><script src="js/maybe-consumer.js"></script></body></html>');
    42	  fs.writeFileSync(path.join(dir, 'main.js'), '// no R3 phase consumer\n');
    43	  fs.writeFileSync(path.join(dir, 'preload.js'), '// no preload consumer\n');
    44	  return dir;
    45	}
    46	
    47	// ── PASS: real repo ──
    48	// Per-phase consumer authorization: the validator's PASS posture is "every consumer is listed in
    49	// state.authorizedProductionPaths". The real repo may carry 0..N authorized consumers depending on
    50	// where each phase's currentCheckpoint sits — assert unauthorizedConsumerCount===0 instead of
    51	// runtimeConsumerCount===0 (which would falsely fail for R3.0D after D2 advances).
    52	for (const phase of PHASES) {
    53	  const r = runValidator(phase, null);
    54	  chk(phase + ' PASS real repo rc=0', r.status === 0, { status: r.status, violations: r.artifact && r.artifact.violations });
    55	  chk(phase + ' PASS unauthorizedConsumerCount=0', r.artifact && r.artifact.unauthorizedConsumerCount === 0);
    56	  chk(phase + ' PASS every consumer is authorized (consumers === authorizedConsumers)', r.artifact && r.artifact.runtimeConsumerCount === r.artifact.authorizedConsumerCount);
    57	  chk(phase + ' PASS contractPrefix=' + CONTRACT_PREFIX[phase], r.artifact && r.artifact.contractPrefix === CONTRACT_PREFIX[phase]);
    58	}
    59	
    60	// ── FAIL: missing R3_PHASE_PROGRAM ──
    61	{
    62	  const tmp = fs.mkdtempSync(path.join(os.tmpdir(), 'r3-phase-noc-art-'));
    63	  const env = Object.assign({}, process.env, { ARTIFACT_DIR: tmp });
    64	  delete env.R3_PHASE_PROGRAM;
    65	  const r = cp.spawnSync('node', [SCRIPT], { cwd: REPO, encoding: 'utf8', env });
    66	  let artifact = null;
    67	  try { artifact = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(path.join(tmp, 'r3-phase-no-consumer.json'), 'utf8')); } catch (_) { artifact = null; }
    68	  chk('FAIL missing R3_PHASE_PROGRAM rc=1', r.status === 1);
    69	  chk('FAIL missing R3_PHASE_PROGRAM artifact PHASE_PROGRAM_INVALID', artifact && Array.isArray(artifact.violations) && artifact.violations.some(v => v.code === 'PHASE_PROGRAM_INVALID'));
    70	}
    71	
    72	// ── FAIL: synthetic renderer/js/ file requires contracts/<phase>/ ──
    73	// The synthetic file (renderer/js/maybe-consumer.js) is NEVER in state.authorizedProductionPaths
    74	// for any phase (the real repo's state only authorizes the actual phase production paths). So a
    75	// synthetic consumer is always flagged as PROD_UNAUTHORIZED_CONSUMER.
    76	for (const phase of PHASES) {
    77	  const prefix = CONTRACT_PREFIX[phase];
    78	  const tree = buildSyntheticTree('var c = require("../../' + prefix + '/index.js");\nmodule.exports = c;\n');
    79	  const r = runValidator(phase, tree);
    80	  chk(phase + ' FAIL synthetic consumer rc!=0', r.status !== 0);
    81	  chk(phase + ' FAIL synthetic consumer detected', r.artifact && r.artifact.runtimeConsumerCount > 0);
    82	  chk(phase + ' FAIL synthetic consumer PROD_UNAUTHORIZED_CONSUMER', r.artifact && r.artifact.violations.some(v => v.code === 'PROD_UNAUTHORIZED_CONSUMER'));
    83	  chk(phase + ' FAIL synthetic consumer unauthorizedConsumerCount>0', r.artifact && r.artifact.unauthorizedConsumerCount > 0);
    84	}
    85	
    86	// ── FAIL: synthetic index.html loads contracts/<phase>/ script ──
    87	for (const phase of PHASES) {
    88	  const prefix = CONTRACT_PREFIX[phase];
    89	  const dir = fs.mkdtempSync(path.join(os.tmpdir(), 'r3-phase-noc-html-'));
    90	  fs.mkdirSync(path.join(dir, 'renderer', 'js'), { recursive: true });
    91	  fs.mkdirSync(path.join(dir, 'renderer'), { recursive: true });
    92	  fs.writeFileSync(path.join(dir, 'renderer', 'js', 'clean.js'), '// clean\n');
    93	  fs.writeFileSync(path.join(dir, 'renderer', 'index.html'), '<!doctype html><html><body><script src="../' + prefix + '/x.js"></script></body></html>');
    94	  const r = runValidator(phase, dir);

   155	  // Codex D1 R2 Finding RN-06 closure: shared Proxy-rejection input cleanser. Every main validator
   156	  // (validateSourceIdentity / validateEvidenceNodeShape / validateHypothesisShape /
   157	  // validateRecommendationShape / validateEngineerBriefShape / validateDecisionInputShape) calls
   158	  // _toCleanCopy AT ENTRY. The result is a plain-prototype deep clone — a Proxy whose ownKeys/get
   159	  // traps lie about hidden state CANNOT survive: structuredClone enumerates via the engine's internal
   160	  // [[OwnPropertyKeys]] + [[GetOwnProperty]] traps (same surface), but the resulting clone is a
   161	  // PLAIN OBJECT whose own-property set is exactly what the Proxy reported. Downstream validators
   162	  // operating on the clone therefore see EXACTLY what they validate — no parallel-universe hidden
   163	  // properties remain reachable via direct property access on the original. structuredClone throw
   164	  // (DataCloneError on functions / symbols-as-values / shared array buffers / etc.) → fail-closed.
   165	  // JSON round-trip fallback when structuredClone unavailable: same posture but cannot preserve
   166	  // certain shapes; fail-closed on any throw.
   167	  function _toCleanCopy(v) {
   168	    if (v === null || v === undefined) return v;
   169	    if (typeof v !== 'object') return v;
   170	    // Codex D1 R3 Finding RN-11 closure: NO JSON.stringify fallback. The previous fallback invoked
   171	    // inherited toJSON() methods on class instances, silently dropped Symbol values + functions +
   172	    // undefined, and could transform a hostile non-plain object into a validator-approved plain
   173	    // shape. Modern runtimes (Node 17+, browsers ~2022+) have structuredClone built in; when it is
   174	    // unavailable the contract fails closed (returns null → caller treats as "not a plain object").
   175	    if (typeof structuredClone !== 'function') return null;
   176	    try { return structuredClone(v); } catch (e) { return null; }
   177	  }
   178	  /**
   179	   * _hasHiddenOwnKey(v) — detects Symbol-keyed OR non-enumerable own properties on the TOP-LEVEL
   180	   * of v. structuredClone silently drops both, so without this check a value like { caseId: 'x',
   181	   * [hostileSym]: payload } would clone to { caseId: 'x' } and pass validation. The pre-clone check
   182	   * rejects such inputs at the boundary so the failure surface mirrors the Codex D1 R1 RN-01
   183	   * recommended behaviour exactly. Throws (Proxy traps lying) → treat as hidden (fail closed).
   184	   */
   185	  function _hasHiddenOwnKey(v) {
   186	    if (v === null || typeof v !== 'object') return false;
   187	    try {
   188	      if (typeof Object.getOwnPropertySymbols === 'function' && Object.getOwnPropertySymbols(v).length > 0) return true;
   189	      var allKeys = Object.getOwnPropertyNames(v);
   190	      var enumKeys = Object.keys(v);
   191	      if (allKeys.length !== enumKeys.length) return true;
   192	    } catch (e) { return true; }
   193	    return false;
   194	  }
   195	  /**
   196	   * _isOriginalPlainObject(v) — Codex D1 R4 Finding RN-11 closure: prototype check on the ORIGINAL
   197	   * input BEFORE structuredClone runs. structuredClone happily converts an ordinary class instance
   198	   * into a plain object copy (DataCloneError only on a narrower set: functions, Symbol values,
   199	   * SharedArrayBuffer with shared:true, etc.), which means a class instance with valid own fields
   200	   * + an inherited toJSON method could otherwise pass _isPlain AFTER cloning even though the
   201	   * ORIGINAL was a class instance. Calling this check BEFORE toCleanCopy in each main validator
   202	   * makes class instances fail-closed at the boundary regardless of their own-field shape.
   203	   */
   204	  function _isOriginalPlainObject(v) {
   205	    if (v === null || typeof v !== 'object' || Array.isArray(v)) return false;
   206	    try { var p = Object.getPrototypeOf(v); return p === Object.prototype || p === null; }
   207	    catch (e) { return false; }
   208	  }
   209	  /**
   210	   * _hasNonPlainNestedObject(v, depth) — Codex D1 R5 Finding RN-11 (nested-level) closure:
   211	   * recursively walks the ORIGINAL input pre-clone and returns true if ANY nested object (at any
   212	   * depth, including inside arrays) has a prototype that is not Object.prototype / null / Array.
   213	   * Catches class instances embedded as identity / confidence / observation / nested entry values.
   214	   * Also rejects accessor descriptors at any level — a hostile getter at depth would otherwise be
   215	   * invoked by structuredClone's [[Get]] semantics. Symbol keys are silently skipped here (the
   216	   * top-level hasHiddenOwnKey already rejects them at the boundary; descending into Symbol-keyed
   217	   * subtrees would itself invoke their getters). Depth cap 32 prevents pathological recursion.
   218	   */
   219	  function _hasNonPlainNestedObject(v, depth) {
   220	    if (depth == null) depth = 0;
   221	    if (depth > 32) return true;
   222	    if (v === null || typeof v !== 'object') return false;
   223	    var isArr = Array.isArray(v);
   224	    // Codex D1 R6 Finding RN-11 (array subclass) closure: arrays must be EXACTLY Array.prototype-
   225	    // proto. An `Array` subclass instance OR an array with a mutated prototype (Object.setPrototypeOf
   226	    // applied) passes Array.isArray() but does NOT have the standard array prototype; reject those
   227	    // with the same posture as a class instance.
   228	    if (isArr) {
   229	      try { var ap = Object.getPrototypeOf(v); if (ap !== Array.prototype) return true; }
   230	      catch (e) { return true; }
   231	    } else {
   232	      try { var p = Object.getPrototypeOf(v); if (!(p === Object.prototype || p === null)) return true; }
   233	      catch (e) { return true; }
   234	    }
   235	    try {
   236	      var keys = Reflect.ownKeys(v);
   237	      for (var i = 0; i < keys.length; i++) {
   238	        var k = keys[i];
   239	        if (typeof k === 'symbol') return true; // any nested symbol key — fail closed
   240	        var d;
   241	        try { d = Object.getOwnPropertyDescriptor(v, k); } catch (e) { return true; }
   242	        if (!d) continue;
   243	        if (typeof d.get === 'function' || typeof d.set === 'function') return true; // accessor descriptor
   244	        if (_hasNonPlainNestedObject(d.value, depth + 1)) return true;
   245	      }
   246	    } catch (e) { return true; }
   247	    return false;
   248	  }

    62	  // NodeId grammar. Directive: "no private path, no filename, no raw telemetry, deterministic / secure
    63	  // immutable id, caller cannot collide". The contract enforces a grammar (no '/', no '..', no '\\',
    64	  // no leading '.'); production-side D2 layer adds collision detection.
    65	  var NODE_ID_RE = /^[A-Za-z0-9][A-Za-z0-9._-]{0,127}$/;
    66	  var NODE_ID_FORBIDDEN_RE = /(\.\.|[\/\\]|^\.)/;
    67	
    68	  function _isPlain(v) { if (v == null || typeof v !== 'object' || Array.isArray(v)) return false; try { var p = Object.getPrototypeOf(v); return p === Object.prototype || p === null; } catch (e) { return false; } }
    69	  function _nonEmptyStr(v) { return typeof v === 'string' && v.length > 0; }
    70	  // Codex D1 R1 Finding RN-01 closure: Reflect.ownKeys catches non-enumerable + Symbol-keyed.
    71	  function _hasOnlyAllowedKeys(o, allowed) { var keys; try { keys = Reflect.ownKeys(o); } catch (e) { return false; } for (var i = 0; i < keys.length; i++) { var k = keys[i]; if (typeof k === 'symbol') return false; if (allowed.indexOf(k) === -1) return false; } return true; }
    72	  function _isFiniteNum(v) { return typeof v === 'number' && v === v && v !== Infinity && v !== -Infinity; }
    73	  function _utf8Bytes(s) { try { return (typeof TextEncoder !== 'undefined') ? new TextEncoder().encode(s).length : Buffer.byteLength(s, 'utf8'); } catch (e) { return (typeof s === 'string') ? s.length * 4 : 0; } }
    74	  function _isStringCodeArray(v, cap) { if (!Array.isArray(v) || v.length > cap) return false; for (var i = 0; i < v.length; i++) if (typeof v[i] !== 'string' || v[i].length === 0) return false; return true; }
    75	
    76	  /**
    77	   * validateObservationShape(o) — closed-key plain object with kind ∈ OBSERVATION_KIND_ALLOWED,
    78	   * i18nKey non-empty string (no UI prose at D1), optional params plain object whose own values are
    79	   * finite numbers, booleans, null, or short strings (≤ PARAMS_VALUE_BYTE_CAP).
    80	   */
    81	  function validateObservationShape(o) {
    82	    // RN-02 closure: outer try/catch.
    83	    try {
    84	    if (!_isPlain(o)) return RC.buildBlockedResult([CODES.EVIDENCE_OBSERVATION_INVALID], { detail: 'observation not a plain object' });
    85	    if (!_hasOnlyAllowedKeys(o, OBSERVATION_KEYS)) return RC.buildBlockedResult([CODES.EVIDENCE_OBSERVATION_INVALID, CODES.UNKNOWN_OWN_KEY]);
    86	    if (OBSERVATION_KIND_ALLOWED.indexOf(o.kind) === -1) return RC.buildBlockedResult([CODES.EVIDENCE_OBSERVATION_INVALID], { detail: 'observation.kind not allowed' });
    87	    if (!_nonEmptyStr(o.i18nKey)) return RC.buildBlockedResult([CODES.EVIDENCE_OBSERVATION_INVALID], { detail: 'observation.i18nKey missing' });
    88	    if (_utf8Bytes(o.i18nKey) > STRING_BYTE_CAP) return RC.buildBlockedResult([CODES.BYTE_CAP_EXCEEDED]);
    89	    if ('channel' in o && o.channel !== null && !_nonEmptyStr(o.channel)) return RC.buildBlockedResult([CODES.EVIDENCE_OBSERVATION_INVALID]);
    90	    if ('params' in o && o.params !== null && o.params !== undefined) {
    91	      if (!_isPlain(o.params)) return RC.buildBlockedResult([CODES.EVIDENCE_OBSERVATION_INVALID]);
    92	      // Codex D1 R2 Finding RN-07 closure: Reflect.ownKeys + Symbol rejection mirrors validateParamsShape.
    93	      var pk; try { pk = Reflect.ownKeys(o.params); } catch (e) { return RC.buildBlockedResult([CODES.EVIDENCE_OBSERVATION_INVALID]); }
    94	      for (var i = 0; i < pk.length; i++) {
    95	        var k = pk[i];
    96	        if (typeof k === 'symbol') return RC.buildBlockedResult([CODES.EVIDENCE_OBSERVATION_INVALID, CODES.UNKNOWN_OWN_KEY]);
    97	        var v = o.params[k];
    98	        if (v === null || typeof v === 'boolean') continue;
    99	        if (typeof v === 'number') { if (!_isFiniteNum(v)) return RC.buildBlockedResult([CODES.NUMERIC_INVALID]); continue; }
   100	        if (typeof v === 'string') { if (_utf8Bytes(v) > PARAMS_VALUE_BYTE_CAP) return RC.buildBlockedResult([CODES.BYTE_CAP_EXCEEDED]); continue; }
   101	        return RC.buildBlockedResult([CODES.EVIDENCE_OBSERVATION_INVALID], { detail: 'observation.params value type not allowed' });
   102	      }
   103	    }
   104	    return Object.freeze({ valid: true });
   105	    } catch (e) {
   106	      return RC.buildBlockedResult([CODES.EVIDENCE_OBSERVATION_INVALID, CODES.INTERNAL_CONTRACT_VIOLATION], { detail: 'observation validator threw on hostile input' });
   107	    }
   108	  }
   109	
   110	  /**
   111	   * validateEvidenceNodeShape(n) — D1 STRUCTURAL gate. Composes credibility, source-identity, and
   112	   * observation validators. Returns { valid:true } or buildBlockedResult.
   113	   *
   114	   * D1 does NOT validate the graph relations (cycles, orphans, double-counting) — that is the D2
   115	   * EVIDENCE_GRAPH service's job. D1 ONLY proves a single node's STRUCTURE.

   117	  function validateEvidenceNodeShape(nIn) {
   118	    // Outer try/catch + Codex D1 R2 RN-06 Proxy-rejection input clone.
   119	    try {
   120	    if (!RC.isOriginalPlainObject(nIn)) return RC.buildBlockedResult([CODES.EVIDENCE_NODE_INVALID, CODES.PROTOTYPE_POLLUTION_REJECTED], { detail: 'node prototype is not Object.prototype or null (class instance / Proxy / non-plain rejected pre-clone)' });
   121	    if (RC.hasHiddenOwnKey(nIn)) return RC.buildBlockedResult([CODES.EVIDENCE_NODE_INVALID, CODES.UNKNOWN_OWN_KEY], { detail: 'node carries Symbol-keyed or non-enumerable own property' });
   122	    if (RC.hasNonPlainNestedObject(nIn)) return RC.buildBlockedResult([CODES.EVIDENCE_NODE_INVALID, CODES.PROTOTYPE_POLLUTION_REJECTED], { detail: 'node contains a nested non-plain object (class instance laundered through identity / confidence / observation)' });
   123	    var n = RC.toCleanCopy(nIn);
   124	    if (!_isPlain(n)) return RC.buildBlockedResult([CODES.EVIDENCE_NODE_INVALID], { detail: 'node not a plain object (or proxy/non-cloneable rejected)' });
   125	    if (!_hasOnlyAllowedKeys(n, EVIDENCE_NODE_KEYS)) return RC.buildBlockedResult([CODES.EVIDENCE_NODE_INVALID, CODES.UNKNOWN_OWN_KEY]);
   126	    var reasons = [];
   127	
   128	    // schemaVersion: must be the integer SUPPORTED_SCHEMA_VERSION. Higher → UNSUPPORTED_FUTURE_SCHEMA.
   129	    if (!Number.isInteger(n.schemaVersion)) reasons.push(CODES.EVIDENCE_NODE_INVALID);
   130	    else if (n.schemaVersion > SUPPORTED_SCHEMA_VERSION) reasons.push(CODES.UNSUPPORTED_FUTURE_SCHEMA);
   131	    else if (n.schemaVersion < 1) reasons.push(CODES.EVIDENCE_NODE_INVALID);
   132	
   133	    // nodeId
   134	    if (!_nonEmptyStr(n.nodeId)) reasons.push(CODES.EVIDENCE_NODE_MISSING_ID);
   135	    else if (NODE_ID_FORBIDDEN_RE.test(n.nodeId) || !NODE_ID_RE.test(n.nodeId)) reasons.push(CODES.EVIDENCE_NODE_ID_FORBIDDEN);
   136

   138	    if (EVIDENCE_CATEGORIES.indexOf(n.category) === -1) reasons.push(CODES.EVIDENCE_CATEGORY_UNKNOWN);
   139

   142	    if (idCheck.valid !== true) reasons.push.apply(reasons, idCheck.reasonCodes || [CODES.SOURCE_IDENTITY_INVALID]);
   143	
   144	    // credibility, provenance, availability
   145	    var crCheck = CR.validateEvidenceCredibility(n.credibility);
   146	    if (crCheck.valid !== true) reasons.push(CODES.EVIDENCE_CREDIBILITY_INVALID);
   147	    var prCheck = CR.validateProvenance(n.provenance);
   148	    if (prCheck.valid !== true) reasons.push(CODES.EVIDENCE_PROVENANCE_INVALID);
   149	    var avCheck = CR.validateAvailability(n.availability);
   150	    if (avCheck.valid !== true) reasons.push(CODES.EVIDENCE_OBSERVATION_INVALID);
   151

   153	    var cfCheck = CR.validateConfidenceShape(n.confidence);
   154	    if (cfCheck.valid !== true) reasons.push(CODES.EVIDENCE_CONFIDENCE_FORBIDDEN);

   169	      }

   171

   174	    if (!_isStringCodeArray(n.contradictingEdges, EDGES_ARRAY_CAP)) reasons.push(CODES.ARRAY_CAP_EXCEEDED);
   175	
   176	    // self-reference (D1 catches the easy case; D2 catches deep cycles)

codex
The closure commit has at least three reproducible gaps: the clock can still mutate unsnapshotted array contents, canonical sorting omits `confidence.state`, and the scanner misses constructed phase strings. I’m now validating their exact observable effects and checking whether the byte cap is early enough to provide the claimed resource bound.

    72	   *   • freshness not ISO-8601 shape   → SOURCE_IDENTITY_INVALID
    73	   */
    74	  function validateSourceIdentity(idIn) {
    75	    // Codex D1 R1 Finding RN-02 + R2 Finding RN-06 closure: outer try/catch AND Proxy-rejection input
    76	    // clone. The validator operates on the cleaned clone, not the original — a Proxy that hides keys
    77	    // is reduced to its disclosed surface, then the clone is itself a plain object whose own-property
    78	    // set is fully enumerable. structuredClone DataCloneError → fail-closed.
    79	    try {
    80	    if (!RC.isOriginalPlainObject(idIn)) return RC.buildBlockedResult([CODES.SOURCE_IDENTITY_INVALID, CODES.PROTOTYPE_POLLUTION_REJECTED], { detail: 'identity prototype is not Object.prototype or null (class instance / Proxy / non-plain rejected pre-clone)' });
    81	    if (RC.hasHiddenOwnKey(idIn)) return RC.buildBlockedResult([CODES.SOURCE_IDENTITY_INVALID, CODES.UNKNOWN_OWN_KEY], { detail: 'identity carries Symbol-keyed or non-enumerable own property' });
    82	    if (RC.hasNonPlainNestedObject(idIn)) return RC.buildBlockedResult([CODES.SOURCE_IDENTITY_INVALID, CODES.PROTOTYPE_POLLUTION_REJECTED], { detail: 'identity contains a nested non-plain object (class instance / accessor / nested Symbol key)' });
    83	    var id = RC.toCleanCopy(idIn);
    84	    if (!_isPlain(id)) return RC.buildBlockedResult([CODES.INTERNAL_CONTRACT_VIOLATION], { detail: 'identity not a plain object (or proxy/non-cloneable rejected)' });
    85	    if (!_hasOnlyAllowedKeys(id, SOURCE_IDENTITY_KEYS)) return RC.buildBlockedResult([CODES.SOURCE_IDENTITY_INVALID, CODES.UNKNOWN_OWN_KEY], { detail: 'identity carries forbidden own key' });
    86	    var reasons = [];
    87	    SOURCE_IDENTITY_REQUIRED_KEYS.forEach(function (k) {
    88	      var v = id[k];
    89	      if (!_nonEmptyStr(v)) { reasons.push(CODES.SOURCE_IDENTITY_INVALID); return; }
    90	      if (_utf8Bytes(v) > STRING_BYTE_CAP) reasons.push(CODES.BYTE_CAP_EXCEEDED);
    91	    });
    92	    if (!('lapId' in id)) {
    93	      // lapId is OPTIONAL — default null when omitted. The producer MAY omit it for case/session-scoped
    94	      // evidence; consumers MUST treat absence as null.
    95	    } else if (id.lapId !== null) {
    96	      if (!_nonEmptyStr(id.lapId)) reasons.push(CODES.SOURCE_IDENTITY_LAP_MISMATCH);
    97	      else if (_utf8Bytes(id.lapId) > STRING_BYTE_CAP) reasons.push(CODES.BYTE_CAP_EXCEEDED);
    98	    }
    99	    if (_nonEmptyStr(id.freshness) && !ISO8601_RE.test(id.freshness)) reasons.push(CODES.SOURCE_IDENTITY_INVALID);
   100	    if (!_nonEmptyStr(id.sourceVersion)) {
   101	      // Already caught above as SOURCE_IDENTITY_INVALID — keep the specific code too for diagnostic value.
   102	      reasons.push(CODES.SOURCE_IDENTITY_VERSION_MISSING);
   103	    }
   104	    if (reasons.length) {
   105	      var seen = {}, out = [];
   106	      reasons.forEach(function (c) { if (!seen[c]) { seen[c] = true; out.push(c); } });
   107	      return RC.buildBlockedResult(out);
   108	    }
   109	    return Object.freeze({
   110	      valid: true,
   111	      identity: Object.freeze({
   112	        caseId: id.caseId,
   113	        sessionId: id.sessionId,
   114	        lapId: ('lapId' in id) ? id.lapId : null,
   115	        sourceId: id.sourceId,
   116	        sourceVersion: id.sourceVersion,
   117	        freshness: id.freshness,
   118	      }),
   119	    });
   120	    } catch (e) {
   121	      return RC.buildBlockedResult([CODES.SOURCE_IDENTITY_INVALID, CODES.INTERNAL_CONTRACT_VIOLATION], { detail: 'source-identity validator threw on hostile input' });
   122	    }
   123	  }
